body: Edited: since my post I have been in contact with management. They apologized for the service and lack of eggs. They have also offered  to make it up to us at another time. I appreciate management making  the time to make it right.
Original: We made a reservation for brunch. A well advertised and recommended brunch. When we arrived we were given upstairs or downstairs seating.  We chose upstairs to enjoy  the view and music. Live saxophone music and a beautiful view was the perfect ending  to a holiday weekend.  We were not greeted upstairs and quickly realized they were understaffed.  A gentleman bussed the table for us and was obviously aggravated.  The table next to it even commented on the aggression of  the table cleaning. He threw plates, silverware,  and glasses into the tub. Several items fell to the floor. Syrup was slung across the table and an attempt to clean it was made. We had to do this again before we could sit. It was on the side of the table and the wall. We were soon met with a waiter that explained the short staffing and no eggs. Yes, that is  no eggs left, ran out of eggs. So our brunch was changed to an early lunch. We still enjoyed the music, scenery, and mimosas. The food we ate was good, it was not brunch and not what we went for. I am not sure if we recommended this place to our friends,  but definitely not for brunch.
